{"forms":[{"form":"conchoids","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The conchoid of a circle with respect to a point on the circle is a cardioid if the fixed distance is equal to the diameter of the circle.","type":"example"},{"text":"The Conchoid of Nicomedes is the conchoid of a straight line with respect to a point not on the line.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any of a family of curves defined as the locus of points p, such that each p is on a line that passes through a given fixed point P and intersects a given curve, C, and the distance from p to the point of intersection with C is a specified constant (note that for nontrivial cases two such points p satisfy the criteria, and the resultant curve has two parts)."],"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A conchoidal fracture in rock."],"topics":["geography","geology","natural-sciences"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":2766147,"upstream_offset":8148874225,"upstream_record_sha256":"b849fff8605bd7aff86027287ba6976b55e2580120d3c070c0a13ca9bfba4290","word":"conchoid"}
